##### Raw second transcription

```yaml
```yaml
lines:
  - id: k1s.h_zero
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Zero"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: sales.zero}

  - id: k1s.h_out_of_scope
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This Schedule K-1 has an item or situation this tool does not compute (boxes 6, 9, 10, 11, 12, 13 other than code O, 14, 15, 16 E/F, 17 other codes, more than one activity, disposition, fiscal year, prior-year basis, at-risk or credit carryovers, or debt-financed acquisition interest)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.multi, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.disposed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.fiscal, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.box6, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.box9, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.box10, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.box11_12, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.box13_other, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.box14, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.box15, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.basis_carry, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.risk_carry, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.credit_carry, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.interest_buy, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [k1s.box16_any, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [k1s.box16ef, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [k1s.box17_any, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [k1s.box17_other, {const: true}]}

  # ---- Box amounts, zero when the box is empty ----
  - id: k1s.h_b1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 1 ordinary business income (loss), zero if empty"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box1_any, {const: true}]}, then: k1s.box1}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box1_any, {const: false}]}, then: k1s.h_zero}
  - id: k1s.h_b2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 2 net rental real estate income (loss), zero if empty"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box2_any, {const: true}]}, then: k1s.box2}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box2_any, {const: false}]}, then: k1s.h_zero}
  - id: k1s.h_b3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 3 other net rental income (loss), zero if empty"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box3_any, {const: true}]}, then: k1s.box3}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box3_any, {const: false}]}, then: k1s.h_zero}
  - id: k1s.h_b1_pos
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 1 income"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: k1s.h_b1, take: {const: 0}}
  - id: k1s.h_b1_neg
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 1 loss, as a positive amount"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: k1s.h_zero, take: k1s.h_b1}
  - id: k1s.h_b2_pos
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 2 income"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: k1s.h_b2, take: {const: 0}}
  - id: k1s.h_b2_neg
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 2 loss, as a positive amount"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: k1s.h_zero, take: k1s.h_b2}
  - id: k1s.h_b3_pos
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 3 income"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: k1s.h_b3, take: {const: 0}}
  - id: k1s.h_b3_neg
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 3 loss, as a positive amount"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: k1s.h_zero, take: k1s.h_b3}

  # ---- At-risk screen ----
  - id: k1s.h_any_loss
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This Schedule K-1 reports a loss in box 1, 2, 3, 7 or 8a"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, gt: [k1s.h_b1_neg, {const: 0}]}
        - {op: test, gt: [k1s.h_b2_neg, {const: 0}]}
        - {op: test, gt: [k1s.h_b3_neg, {const: 0}]}
        - {op: test, lt: [k1s.st, {const: 0}]}
        - {op: test, lt: [k1s.lt, {const: 0}]}
  - id: k1s.h_risk
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "A loss with amounts not at risk (Form 6198 needed)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_any_loss, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.not_at_risk, {const: true}]}

  # ---- Basis limitation (Form 7203, Part III, column (e)) ----
  - id: k1s.h_e35
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 7203 line 35 column (e): ordinary business loss in excess of basis"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f7203, field: f7203.e35}
    note: "The supplied text does not print the Part III line captions; lines 35 to 38 are read as the ordinary business, net rental real estate, other net rental and net capital loss lines, matching the order of boxes 1, 2, 3 and 7/8. With no Form 7203 entry, nothing is disallowed."
  - id: k1s.h_e36
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 7203 line 36 column (e): net rental real estate loss in excess of basis"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f7203, field: f7203.e36}
  - id: k1s.h_e37
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 7203 line 37 column (e): other net rental loss in excess of basis"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f7203, field: f7203.e37}
  - id: k1s.h_e38
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 7203 line 38 column (e): net capital loss in excess of basis"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f7203, field: f7203.e38}
  - id: k1s.h_cap_limited
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Part of the capital loss is limited by basis"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [k1s.h_e38, {const: 0}]}
  - id: k1s.h_b1_loss
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 1 loss after the basis limitation"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: k1s.h_b1_neg, take: k1s.h_e35}
    refuses: [k1s.h_risk]
  - id: k1s.h_b2_loss
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 2 loss after the basis limitation"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: k1s.h_b2_neg, take: k1s.h_e36}
    refuses: [k1s.h_risk]
  - id: k1s.h_b3_loss
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 3 loss after the basis limitation"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: k1s.h_b3_neg, take: k1s.h_e37}
    refuses: [k1s.h_risk]

  # ---- Passive / nonpassive determination ----
  - id: k1s.h_material
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "You materially participated in the corporation's trade or business activity"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [k1s.work, {const: true}]}
            - op: test
              any:
                - {op: test, eq: [k1s.hours500, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [k1s.most_hours, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [k1s.mp_other, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.past_years, {const: true}]}
    note: "Tests 5 and 6 (earlier years) do not require work in 2025, so past_years is tested on its own; tests 1 to 4 and 7 require work in 2025."
  - id: k1s.h_b1_np
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 1 is nonpassive (material participation, or trading personal property for owners)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_material, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.trader, {const: true}]}
  - id: k1s.h_b2_np
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 2 is nonpassive (real estate professional who materially participated, or a dwelling unit used personally)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [k1s.re_pro, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_material, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.dwelling, {const: true}]}
    note: "The dwelling-unit rule is applied to box 2 only (rental homes are rental real estate); box 3 is treated as passive for all shareholders, as its instructions say."
  - id: k1s.h_mfs_together
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Married filing separately and lived with spouse at some time in the year"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfs"}]}
        - {op: test, ne: [household.mfs_living, {const: "apart_all_year"}]}
  - id: k1s.h_part4
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Active participation in the rental real estate (Form 8582, Part IV)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.active, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.tenth, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_mfs_together, {const: false}]}
  - id: k1s.h_b2_p4
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 2 is passive and goes in Form 8582, Part IV"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b2_np, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_part4, {const: true}]}
  - id: k1s.h_b2_p5
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 2 is passive and goes in Form 8582, Part V"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b2_np, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_part4, {const: false}]}

  # ---- Box 1 split ----
  - id: k1s.h_b1_p_inc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 1 passive income"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b1_np, {const: true}]}, then: k1s.h_zero}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b1_np, {const: false}]}, then: k1s.h_b1_pos}
  - id: k1s.h_b1_p_loss
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 1 passive loss"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b1_np, {const: true}]}, then: k1s.h_zero}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b1_np, {const: false}]}, then: k1s.h_b1_loss}
  - id: k1s.h_b1_np_inc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 1 nonpassive income"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b1_np, {const: true}]}, then: k1s.h_b1_pos}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b1_np, {const: false}]}, then: k1s.h_zero}
  - id: k1s.h_b1_np_loss
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 1 nonpassive loss"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b1_np, {const: true}]}, then: k1s.h_b1_loss}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b1_np, {const: false}]}, then: k1s.h_zero}

  # ---- Box 2 split ----
  - id: k1s.h_b2_np_inc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 2 nonpassive income"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b2_np, {const: true}]}, then: k1s.h_b2_pos}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b2_np, {const: false}]}, then: k1s.h_zero}
  - id: k1s.h_b2_np_loss
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 2 nonpassive loss"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b2_np, {const: true}]}, then: k1s.h_b2_loss}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b2_np, {const: false}]}, then: k1s.h_zero}
  - id: k1s.h_b2_p4_inc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 2 income for Form 8582, Part IV"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b2_p4, {const: true}]}, then: k1s.h_b2_pos}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b2_p4, {const: false}]}, then: k1s.h_zero}
  - id: k1s.h_b2_p4_loss
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 2 loss for Form 8582, Part IV"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b2_p4, {const: true}]}, then: k1s.h_b2_loss}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b2_p4, {const: false}]}, then: k1s.h_zero}
  - id: k1s.h_b2_p5_inc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 2 income for Form 8582, Part V"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b2_p5, {const: true}]}, then: k1s.h_b2_pos}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b2_p5, {const: false}]}, then: k1s.h_zero}
  - id: k1s.h_b2_p5_loss
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 2 loss for Form 8582, Part V"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b2_p5, {const: true}]}, then: k1s.h_b2_loss}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b2_p5, {const: false}]}, then: k1s.h_zero}

  # ---- Prior-year unallowed losses ----
  - id: k1s.h_b1_prior
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Prior-year unallowed loss, box 1 activity (2024 Form 8582, Part VII, column (c))"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.prior_any, {const: true}]}, then: k1s.b1_prior}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.prior_any, {const: false}]}, then: k1s.h_zero}
  - id: k1s.h_b2_prior
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Prior-year unallowed loss, box 2 activity (2024 Form 8582, Part VII, column (c))"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.prior_any, {const: true}]}, then: k1s.b2_prior}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.prior_any, {const: false}]}, then: k1s.h_zero}
  - id: k1s.h_b3_prior
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Prior-year unallowed loss, box 3 activity (2024 Form 8582, Part VII, column (c))"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.prior_any, {const: true}]}, then: k1s.b3_prior}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.prior_any, {const: false}]}, then: k1s.h_zero}
  - id: k1s.h_former_passive
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "A prior-year passive loss from an activity that is nonpassive this year"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b1_np, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, gt: [k1s.h_b1_prior, {const: 0}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b2_np, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, gt: [k1s.h_b2_prior, {const: 0}]}
    note: "The supplied text does not say how a prior-year passive loss of an activity that is nonpassive this year is reported (it points to Schedule E line 27, not supplied); the tool refuses that case."
  - id: k1s.h_b1_prior_p
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 1 prior-year unallowed loss, passive"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b1_np, {const: true}]}, then: k1s.h_zero}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b1_np, {const: false}]}, then: k1s.h_b1_prior}
  - id: k1s.h_b2_prior_in_p4
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 2 prior-year loss stays in Part IV (active participation both in the year it arose and this year)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b2_p4, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, gt: [k1s.h_b2_prior, {const: 0}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.prior_not_active, {const: false}]}
  - id: k1s.h_b2_prior_p4
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 2 prior-year unallowed loss for Form 8582, Part IV, column (c)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b2_prior_in_p4, {const: true}]}, then: k1s.h_b2_prior}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b2_prior_in_p4, {const: false}]}, then: k1s.h_zero}
  - id: k1s.h_b2_prior_to_p5
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 2 prior-year loss goes in Part V"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b2_np, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b2_prior_in_p4, {const: false}]}
  - id: k1s.h_b2_prior_p5
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 2 prior-year unallowed loss for Form 8582, Part V, column (c)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b2_prior_to_p5, {const: true}]}, then: k1s.h_b2_prior}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_b2_prior_to_p5, {const: false}]}, then: k1s.h_zero}

  # ---- Requested printed lines ----
  - id: k1s.col_k
    printed: "28 (k)"
    label: "Schedule E, line 28, column (k): nonpassive income from Schedule K-1"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [k1s.h_b1_np_inc, k1s.h_b2_np_inc]}
    refuses: [k1s.h_out_of_scope, k1s.h_former_passive]
    shown_when: k1s.h_show_k
  - id: k1s.col_i
    printed: "28 (i)"
    label: "Schedule E, line 28, column (i): nonpassive loss from Schedule K-1"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [k1s.h_b1_np_loss, k1s.h_b2_np_loss]}
    refuses: [k1s.h_out_of_scope, k1s.h_former_passive, k1s.h_risk]
    shown_when: k1s.h_show_i
  - id: k1s.col_h
    printed: "28 (h)"
    label: "Schedule E, line 28, column (h): passive income"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [k1s.p4_income, k1s.p5_income]}
    refuses: [k1s.h_out_of_scope, k1s.h_former_passive]
    shown_when: k1s.h_show_h
  - id: k1s.h_show_k
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Column (k) has an amount"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [k1s.col_k, {const: 0}]}
  - id: k1s.h_show_i
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Column (i) has an amount"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [k1s.col_i, {const: 0}]}
  - id: k1s.h_show_h
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Column (h) has an amount"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [k1s.col_h, {const: 0}]}

  # ---- Result interfaces ----
  - id: k1s.p4_income
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8582, Part IV, column (a): current year net income (rental real estate with active participation)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: k1s.h_b2_p4_inc}
    refuses: [k1s.h_out_of_scope]
  - id: k1s.p4_loss
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8582, Part IV, column (b): current year net loss"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: k1s.h_b2_p4_loss}
    refuses: [k1s.h_out_of_scope, k1s.h_risk]
  - id: k1s.p4_prior
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8582, Part IV, column (c): prior-year unallowed loss"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: k1s.h_b2_prior_p4}
    refuses: [k1s.h_out_of_scope]
  - id: k1s.p5_income
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8582, Part V, column (a): current year net income (other passive activities)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [k1s.h_b1_p_inc, k1s.h_b2_p5_inc, k1s.h_b3_pos]}
    refuses: [k1s.h_out_of_scope]
  - id: k1s.p5_loss
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8582, Part V, column (b): current year net loss"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [k1s.h_b1_p_loss, k1s.h_b2_p5_loss, k1s.h_b3_loss]}
    refuses: [k1s.h_out_of_scope, k1s.h_risk]
    note: "Boxes 1, 2 and 3 are each one activity (box 19, more than one activity, is refused); they are added into one Part V figure for this K-1. Losses enter after the basis and at-risk limits, as the instructions order the limitations."
  - id: k1s.p5_prior
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8582, Part V, column (c): prior-year unallowed losses"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [k1s.h_b1_prior_p, k1s.h_b2_prior_p5, k1s.h_b3_prior]}
    refuses: [k1s.h_out_of_scope, k1s.h_former_passive]
  - id: k1s.nonpassive
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Net nonpassive income (loss) from this Schedule K-1 (column (k) less column (i))"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: subtract, from: k1s.col_k, take: k1s.col_i}
    refuses: [k1s.h_out_of_scope, k1s.h_former_passive, k1s.h_risk]
  - id: k1s.int
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 4 interest income (Form 1040, line 2b)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box4_any, {const: true}]}, then: k1s.box4}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box4_any, {const: false}]}, then: k1s.h_zero}
    refuses: [k1s.h_out_of_scope]
  - id: k1s.ord_div
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 5a ordinary dividends (Form 1040, line 3b)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box5_any, {const: true}]}, then: k1s.box5a}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box5_any, {const: false}]}, then: k1s.h_zero}
    refuses: [k1s.h_out_of_scope]
  - id: k1s.qual_div
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 5b qualified dividends (Form 1040, line 3a)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box5_any, {const: true}]}, then: k1s.box5b}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box5_any, {const: false}]}, then: k1s.h_zero}
    refuses: [k1s.h_out_of_scope]
  - id: k1s.st
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 7 net short-term capital gain (loss) (Schedule D, line 5)"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box7_any, {const: true}]}, then: k1s.box7}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box7_any, {const: false}]}, then: k1s.h_zero}
    refuses: [k1s.h_out_of_scope, k1s.h_cap_limited]
    note: "Capital losses are reported after the basis and at-risk limits; when Form 7203 shows part of the net capital loss disallowed (line 38, column (e)) the split between boxes 7 and 8a is not determined by the text and the tool refuses. The at-risk screen is applied to the loss lines that read this one."
  - id: k1s.lt
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 8a net long-term capital gain (loss) (Schedule D, line 12)"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box8_any, {const: true}]}, then: k1s.box8a}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box8_any, {const: false}]}, then: k1s.h_zero}
    refuses: [k1s.h_out_of_scope, k1s.h_cap_limited]
  - id: k1s.g28
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 8b collectibles (28%) gain (loss) (28% Rate Gain Worksheet, line 4)"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box8_any, {const: true}]}, then: k1s.box8b}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box8_any, {const: false}]}, then: k1s.h_zero}
    refuses: [k1s.h_out_of_scope, k1s.h_cap_limited, k1s.h_risk]
  - id: k1s.h_8c_stmt
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 8c points to a statement (other types of unrecaptured section 1250 gain)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.box8_any, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.box8_stmt, {const: true}]}
  - id: k1s.u1250
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 8c unrecaptured section 1250 gain from the corporation's business assets (Unrecaptured Section 1250 Gain Worksheet, line 5)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box8_any, {const: true}]}, then: k1s.box8c}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box8_any, {const: false}]}, then: k1s.h_zero}
    refuses: [k1s.h_out_of_scope, k1s.h_8c_stmt]
  - id: k1s.backup
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 13, code O, backup withholding (Form 1040, line 25c)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box13o_any, {const: true}]}, then: k1s.box13o}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box13o_any, {const: false}]}, then: k1s.h_zero}
    refuses: [k1s.h_out_of_scope]
  - id: k1s.taxexempt
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 16, code A, tax-exempt interest income (Form 1040, line 2a)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box16a_any, {const: true}]}, then: k1s.box16a}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box16a_any, {const: false}]}, then: k1s.h_zero}
    refuses: [k1s.h_out_of_scope]
  - id: k1s.h_v_other
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "The code V statement shows section 199A dividends or qualified PTP income"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.box17v_any, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.v_other, {const: true}]}
  - id: k1s.h_basis_dis
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Losses disallowed by the basis limitation (Form 7203, lines 35 to 38, column (e))"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [k1s.h_e35, k1s.h_e36, k1s.h_e37, k1s.h_e38]}
  - id: k1s.h_passive_loss
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Current-year passive losses from this K-1"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [k1s.p4_loss, k1s.p5_loss]}
  - id: k1s.h_qbi_limited
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Some loss on this K-1 may not be allowed in figuring taxable income this year"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, gt: [k1s.h_basis_dis, {const: 0}]}
        - {op: test, gt: [k1s.h_passive_loss, {const: 0}]}
  - id: k1s.qbi
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 17, code V: qualified business income (loss)"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box17v_any, {const: true}]}, then: k1s.qbi_stmt}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box17v_any, {const: false}]}, then: k1s.h_zero}
    refuses: [k1s.h_out_of_scope, k1s.h_v_other, k1s.h_qbi_limited, k1s.h_risk]
    note: "QBI includes only items allowed in figuring taxable income for the year; how much of a basis-limited or passive loss is allowed is not settled on this K-1 alone, so the statement amount is used only when no loss is basis-disallowed or passive, and the tool refuses otherwise."
```
